# Vellistry - Project Definition

## Working identity
Vellistry is the working name for a proposed author-controlled fiction writing, editing, design, and publishing application with optional AI assistance.

## Product intent
The application should be useful as a conventional manuscript editor even without AI, while enabling writers to guide AI through visual, scoped instructions and supporting the long-term continuity of novels and series.

## Research themes
- Author-focused rich-text and manuscript editing.
- Visual AI instruction/rule configuration, with scoped application and contextual rather than rigid stylistic guidance.
- User-controlled AI provider/API connectivity and separately specified MCP interoperability.
- Character, canon, continuity, plot, and story-state support.
- Authorial-voice preservation and quality assessment.
- Book layout, covers, export, and print/publishing workflows.
- Usability, legal licensing, costs, security, privacy, and portability.

## Scope and boundaries
Fiction and book-writing workflows are the primary focus. Other writing use cases may be evaluated, but are not automatically in scope.

**Accepted manuscript editor foundation:** Tiptap 3 built on ProseMirror ([ADR-0003](docs/decisions/ADR-0003-use-tiptap-3-prosemirror-for-manuscript-editor.md)). The open-source editor core is the selected technology; this approval does not extend to Tiptap's paid pagination/conversion offerings. The broader **Next.js + NestJS + Supabase** implementation baseline was subsequently authorized by the owner and is accepted in ADR-0006 and integrated through merged PR #28. This editor ADR does not itself approve that stack or its production deployment. Publishing/export platform, AI provider, and paid features remain separate decisions.

## Governance
The engineering knowledge base will be versioned in the same Git repository as the future app. The user's manuscript data, credentials, and runtime settings will not automatically be committed to this repository.

See [PROJECT-CONSTITUTION.md](PROJECT-CONSTITUTION.md), [SOURCE-OF-TRUTH.md](SOURCE-OF-TRUTH.md), and [CURRENT-STATE.md](CURRENT-STATE.md).

Current implementation/governance facts live in [CURRENT-STATE.md](CURRENT-STATE.md). [ADR-0008](docs/decisions/ADR-0008-codex-primary-agentic-implementation-workflow.md) records the owner-selected Codex implementer, ChatGPT coordinator/reviewer and owner-only approval/merge boundaries. Bounded coding approvals do not grant private-pilot/release permission.

## Current remediation authority

The default owner approval boundaries remain in force. The [recorded one-time delegation](docs/agent-workflow/authorizations/VEL-REM-INTEGRATION-20261019.yaml) permits only eligible repository remediation integrations during this unfinished assignment, with distinct trusted review and actual CI. It grants no hosted, private-manuscript, release or future-feature authority. [CURRENT-STATE.md](CURRENT-STATE.md) and the [original44-item ledger](docs/reports/VEL-MASTER-REMEDIATION-LEDGER.md) distinguish verified progress from remaining acceptance.

The temporary remediation delegation ends under its [recorded closure condition](docs/agent-workflow/authorizations/VEL-REM-INTEGRATION-20261019-CLOSURE.md). After the final merge/post-main attestation, ordinary owner-governed approval resumes; this exception grants no future feature authority.

# Vellistry Project Constitution

1. **Preserve authorship and intent.** Support writers' decisions, distinctive voices, stylistic irregularities, and deliberate exceptions; avoid indiscriminate generic polishing.
2. **Preserve original sources.** Research summaries do not replace underlying documents; record provenance and distinguish direct quotations, supported findings, inference, and proposals.
3. **Separate evidence from decisions.** AI recommendations and research candidates never become authoritative simply by being recorded.
4. **Require traceability.** Requirements, ADRs, historical events, research records, and experiments receive stable IDs and linked sources or explicit evidence gaps.
5. **Require metadata for decisions and history.** Use YAML front matter, timestamps/date precision, lifecycle states, author/approval attribution, relationships, and supersession links.
6. **Maintain current state.** Update CURRENT-STATE.md whenever a task materially changes agreed progress, decisions, blockers, or next steps.
7. **Use controlled knowledge promotion.** Read authoritative context; submit proposed changes with evidence; verify and approve before marking accepted.
8. **Retain history.** Correct errors through traceable revisions or new corrective events; do not erase prior decisions or rewrite an event as though it never happened.
9. **Remain vendor-neutral.** Models, tools, and orchestrators should be replaceable where practical; document genuine constraints and trade-offs.
10. **Use least-privilege agent access.** Keep secrets out of Git, isolate sensitive/runtime author data, review consequential actions, and require human approval for high-impact changes.
11. **Keep systems proportionate.** Begin with Git/Markdown, lightweight templates, and repeatable manual research; add automated context assembly, durable workflows, and additional stores only if tests justify them.
12. **Version code and knowledge together where appropriate.** Application and its engineering knowledge belong in one repository, while user manuscripts and runtime personal information require separate application storage.

## Status hierarchy
Accepted and effective decisions take precedence over proposals. Superseding decisions must explicitly reference what they replace. Research notes are evidence, not approval.

## Solo-maintainer review policy
Vellistry currently has one human contributor: the project owner. The owner may review and merge their own pull requests after appropriate inspection and required automated checks. A separate human reviewer is **not** a standing requirement during this phase. AI-agent review may provide independent analysis but is advisory, not another human approval. For consequential architecture, security, or production changes, the owner's explicit approval remains required. Revisit the policy when collaborators join. See HIS-20261008-002.

## Change control
Propose amendments in version control with rationale and evidence. Material changes require owner approval, an updated changelog/history event, and a corresponding CURRENT-STATE update.

# Source of Truth and Knowledge Ownership

## Engineering authority
- **Git repository:** canonical engineering documents, code, accepted project decisions, research evidence references, specifications, and change history.
- **CURRENT-STATE.md:** compact, current working status; not a substitute for full evidence.
- **ADRs:** accepted or proposed technical and knowledge-governance decisions, with rationale, alternatives, approval and supersession lineage.
- **Source register:** where original research came from and where an immutable/retrievable copy can be found.
- **Feature inventory:** [docs/features/FEATURE-INVENTORY.md](docs/features/FEATURE-INVENTORY.md) is the reviewable index of discussed feature candidates and source/status links. **It is not an accepted product backlog, release plan, substitute for original source evidence, or approval of proposed features.**
- **Git history and pull requests:** revision provenance; do not substitute automatically for semantic record metadata.
- **ChatGPT and other agents:** analysis and proposal tools, not an independent source of architectural authority.

## Separate runtime authority
Future authors' manuscripts, story canon, application settings, credentials, and stored assets belong in purpose-selected runtime storage, not automatically in the Git engineering knowledge base. The accepted Next.js/NestJS/Supabase baseline is recorded in ADR-0006; the owner subsequently selected Supabase Cloud for development. This is not production hosting or real-manuscript approval; no live database state is inferred from old records.

## Five knowledge layers
1. Authoritative accepted project and implementation knowledge.
2. Current verified implementation/research state.
3. Historical rationale, experiments, rejected/superseded alternatives.
4. Temporary task-local agent context and unapproved hypotheses.
5. Evidence, provenance, authorship, validity, confidence, and traceability.

## Read  Propose  Review  Commit
1. Read the relevant accepted records and source citations.
2. Clearly label new assumptions, findings, recommendations, and uncertainties.
3. Review source fidelity and factual verification independently.
4. Promote material changes only after owner approval; update linked records and current state.
5. When superseding an ADR, preserve the older file and connect both sides of the lineage.

## PR review and sole owner authority
At present the project has a single human contributor. The owner performs self-review and makes the final merge/acceptance decision. A distinct human PR approval is not required. Automated CI and optional AI-agent reviews provide evidence and feedback; agents do not have approval authority. For significant changes, document the owner's review and decision in the PR or associated provenance record. GitHub does not count self-review as a separate approving reviewer; this workflow must not assume otherwise. If branch-protection settings require other human approvals, review the protection policy rather than fabricate reviewers. See HIS-20261008-002.

## Metadata and history
See [docs/governance/RECORD-SCHEMAS.md](docs/governance/RECORD-SCHEMAS.md). All ADR and historical-event records require YAML front matter and meaningful content.

## No silent conflation
The terms *verified evidence*, *accepted requirement*, *approved decision*, *implemented*, and *tested* are different. Never mark one as another without supporting evidence.

## Current task authority and provenance

[AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md) and [ADR-0008](docs/decisions/ADR-0008-codex-primary-agentic-implementation-workflow.md)
express the owner-selected execution roles: ChatGPT coordinates and reviews;
Codex implements authorized bounded tasks; the owner alone approves consequential
scope, adoption and merge. Coordinator write access is not coding permission.
Lightweight triage/indexing/review does not grant authority to change effective
instructions or executable behavior. Any alternate implementer requires an
explicit owner task exception. Git commit author names do not prove runtime identity.

Use the compact [current checkpoint](CURRENT-STATE.md); its dated historical
archive is evidence, not active dispatch. Original source gaps remain gaps.
PR66's prior ChatGPT records are adopted with per-file source SHA attribution,
not relabeled Codex execution. This reconciliation proposes no semantic change
to accepted architecture or owner authority.

## Current remediation authority

The default owner approval boundaries remain in force. The [recorded one-time delegation](docs/agent-workflow/authorizations/VEL-REM-INTEGRATION-20261019.yaml) permits only eligible repository remediation integrations during this unfinished assignment, with distinct trusted review and actual CI. It grants no hosted, private-manuscript, release or future-feature authority. [CURRENT-STATE.md](CURRENT-STATE.md) and the [original44-item ledger](docs/reports/VEL-MASTER-REMEDIATION-LEDGER.md) distinguish verified progress from remaining acceptance.

The temporary remediation delegation ends under its [recorded closure condition](docs/agent-workflow/authorizations/VEL-REM-INTEGRATION-20261019-CLOSURE.md). After the final merge/post-main attestation, ordinary owner-governed approval resumes; this exception grants no future feature authority.

+"use client";
+import React,{useSyncExternalStore} from 'react';
+import {EditorContent} from '@tiptap/react';
+import type {DurableManuscriptComposition} from '../../lib/editor/manuscript-durable-composition';
+/** Unregistered test preview; caller owns controller lifecycle. */
+export function ManuscriptDurablePreview({draft}:{draft:DurableManuscriptComposition}){
+ const local=useSyncExternalStore(draft.subscribe,draft.snapshot,draft.snapshot);
+ const remote=useSyncExternalStore(draft.controller.subscribe,draft.controller.snapshot,draft.controller.snapshot);
+ const session=draft.controller.editorSession;
+ const editorState=useSyncExternalStore(session.subscribe,session.snapshot,session.snapshot);
+ const editor=remote.scope&&editorState.phase==='ready'&&editorState.scope?.bookId===remote.scope.bookId&&editorState.scope.chapterId===remote.scope.chapterId?editorState.editor:null;
+ return <section aria-label="Device draft test preview">
+  <h1>Local draft recovery test</h1>
+  <p>Device storage can be lost. This is not a cross-device backup or application-encrypted storage. Someone using this browser profile may access it. Synthetic writing only.</p>
+  <p role="status" aria-label="Local recovery status" aria-live="polite">{local.message}</p>
+  <p role="status" aria-label="Test server status">{remote.message}</p>
+  <button disabled={!remote.canRecover} onClick={()=>void draft.recover()}>Recover local draft</button>
+  <button disabled={!remote.scope} onClick={()=>void draft.discard()}>Discard local recovery copy</button>
+  <button disabled={!remote.canSave} onClick={()=>void draft.controller.save()}>Save test revision</button>
+  <button disabled={!remote.canReconcile} onClick={()=>void draft.controller.reconcile()}>Check test save result</button>
+  <p>AI off. Manual writing. Local recovery and confirmed test revisions are separate.</p>
+  {editor?<>
+   <div role="group" aria-label="Test manuscript formatting">{(['bold','italic','underline','undo','redo'] as const).map(command=><button key={command} type="button" disabled={!editor.isEditable} onMouseDown={e=>e.preventDefault()} onClick={()=>session.format(command)}>{command.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+command.slice(1)}</button>)}</div>
+   <EditorContent key={editorState.scope!.key} editor={editor}/>
+   <output aria-label="Word count">{editorState.words} words</output>
+  </>:<p>Choose a verified synthetic chapter to write.</p>}
+ </section>;
+}

+import {createManuscriptComposition,type ManuscriptTransport} from './manuscript-composition';
+import {type AsyncDraftStore,type LocalScope,localKey,DraftStorageError} from './manuscript-indexeddb';
+import {saveOperation} from '../../../../api/src/modules/workspace/manuscript-contract';
+import type {WorkspaceSnapshot} from '../workspace/book-workspace-session';
+
+export type LocalPhase='not-stored'|'storing'|'stored'|'unavailable'|'blocked'|'quota'|'aborted'|'corrupt'|'incompatible'|'conflict'|'recovery'|'unverified';
+/** Test-only composition. The existing recovery map is memory; IndexedDB is a separate async port. */
+export function createDurableManuscriptComposition(options:{readOwner:()=>Promise<string|null>;transport:ManuscriptTransport;store:AsyncDraftStore;clientId:string;nextOperationId:()=>string;now?:()=>number}){
+ let epoch=0,disposed=false,active:LocalScope|null=null,sequence=0,write=0,blocked=false;
+ let lastWorkspace:WorkspaceSnapshot|null=null;let queue=Promise.resolve();const memory=new Map<string,unknown>(),hidden=new Set<string>(),mismatched=new Set<string>(),known=new Map<string,number>(),dirty=new Set<string>(),listeners=new Set<()=>void>();
+ let state:Readonly<{phase:LocalPhase;message:string}>=Object.freeze({phase:'unverified' as LocalPhase,message:'Identity unavailable. Local recovery is concealed.'});
+ function publish(phase:LocalPhase,message:string){if(disposed)return;state=Object.freeze({phase,message});listeners.forEach(f=>f());}
+ async function current(e:number,s:LocalScope){if(disposed||epoch!==e||!active||localKey(active)!==localKey(s))return false;let owner:string|null;try{owner=await options.readOwner();}catch{owner=null;}if(disposed||epoch!==e)return false;if(owner!==s.author){pause();return false;}return true;}
+ function failure(e:unknown){const kind=e instanceof DraftStorageError?e.kind:'unavailable';blocked=true;publish(kind,kind==='conflict'?'Another tab changed this recovery copy. Your writing remains in memory; choose the copy explicitly.':'Unable to store a recovery copy. Your current writing remains in memory.');}
+ function schedule(raw:unknown){
+  if(!active||disposed)return;const s=active,e=epoch,n=++write;dirty.add(localKey(active));if(blocked)return;
+  // Clone at dispatch, before any asynchronous work or later editor mutation.
+  const d=structuredClone(raw) as {text:string;base:number;pending?:{id:string;text:string;base:number}};
+  publish('storing','Storing a recovery copy on this device...');
+  queue=queue.then(async()=>{
+   if(!await current(e,s)||blocked)return;
+   try{
+    const pending=d.pending?saveOperation({operationId:d.pending.id,bookId:s.book,chapterId:s.chapter,expectedRevision:d.pending.base,document:JSON.parse(d.pending.text)}):null;
+    const result=await options.store.write(s,sequence,{schema:1,scope:s,timestamp:(options.now??Date.now)(),base:d.base,document:JSON.parse(d.text),pending});
+    known.set(localKey(s),result.sequence);if(JSON.stringify(memory.get(localKey(s)))===JSON.stringify(d))dirty.delete(localKey(s));if(!await current(e,s))return;sequence=result.sequence;
+    if(n===write)publish('stored','Saved on this device, not synced.');
+   }catch(error){if(await current(e,s))failure(error);}
+  });
+ }
+ const transport:ManuscriptTransport={...options.transport,async save(s,operation){const e=epoch,scope=active;if(!scope||!await current(e,scope))throw Error('Identity unavailable');await queue;if(!await current(e,scope)||blocked)throw Error('Pending operation not stored');const durable=await options.store.read(scope);if(!await current(e,scope)||!durable?.pending||JSON.stringify(durable.pending)!==JSON.stringify(operation))throw Error('Pending operation not stored');return options.transport.save(s,operation);},async read(s){
+  const readEpoch=epoch,readScope=active;
+  const result=await options.transport.read(s) as {status:string;value?:{revision:number;document:unknown}};
+  if(readScope&&await current(readEpoch,readScope)&&active&&s.author===active.author&&s.bookId===active.book&&s.chapterId===active.chapter){
+   const k=localKey(active),entry=memory.get(k) as {base:number;text:string;pending?:{base:number;text:string}}|undefined;
+   if(entry&&result.status==='ready'&&result.value){
+    const accepted=result.value;
+    const ackLost=entry.pending&&accepted.revision===entry.pending.base+1&&JSON.stringify(accepted.document)===entry.pending.text;
+    if(entry.base!==accepted.revision&&!ackLost){hidden.add(k);mismatched.add(k);blocked=true;publish('conflict','A recovery copy has a different accepted revision. It was retained without replacing accepted text.');}
+    else if(!entry.pending&&entry.text===JSON.stringify(accepted.document))hidden.add(k);
+   }
+  }
+  return result;
+ }};
+ const controller=createManuscriptComposition({...options,transport,storage:{get:k=>hidden.has(k)?undefined:memory.get(k),set(k,v){hidden.delete(k);memory.set(k,v);schedule(v);}}});
+ // Existing memory key ordering is [author,book,chapter,client], identical to localKey.
+ function pause(){epoch++;active=null;blocked=true;controller.pause();publish('unverified','Identity unavailable. Local recovery is concealed.');}
+ async function bind(workspace:WorkspaceSnapshot){
+  lastWorkspace=workspace;const e=++epoch;active=null;blocked=true;controller.pause();publish('unverified','Checking identity and accepted revision before local recovery...');
+  let owner:string|null;try{owner=await options.readOwner();}catch{owner=null;}
+  if(disposed||e!==epoch)return;
+  if(!owner||!workspace.document||!workspace.selectedChapterId){pause();return;}
+  const s=Object.freeze({author:owner,book:workspace.document.bookId,chapter:workspace.selectedChapterId,client:options.clientId});
+  active=s;sequence=0;write=0;blocked=false;hidden.delete(localKey(s));mismatched.delete(localKey(s));
+  try{
+   const candidate=await options.store.read(s);if(!await current(e,s))return;
+   sequence=candidate?.sequence??0;const k=localKey(s);if(!known.has(k))known.set(k,sequence);if(memory.has(k)&&dirty.has(k)){if(known.has(k)&&known.get(k)!==sequence){failure(new DraftStorageError('conflict'));}else{blocked=true;publish('not-stored','A newer in-memory recovery copy is available. It has not been stored.');}}else{known.set(k,sequence);memory.delete(k);if(candidate&&!candidate.discarded)memory.set(localKey(s),{scope:{author:s.author,book:s.book,chapter:s.chapter},text:JSON.stringify(candidate.document),base:candidate.base,phase:'recoverable',...(candidate.pending?{pending:{id:candidate.pending.operationId,text:JSON.stringify(candidate.pending.document),base:candidate.pending.expectedRevision}}:{})});}
+  }catch(error){if(!await current(e,s))return;failure(error);}
+  if(!await current(e,s))return;await controller.bind(workspace);if(!await current(e,s))return;if(mismatched.has(localKey(s)))controller.editorSession.snapshot().editor?.setEditable(false,false);
+  if(!blocked)publish(controller.snapshot().canRecover?'recovery':'not-stored',controller.snapshot().canRecover?'Recovery copy available. Choose explicitly; accepted text is unchanged.':'Local changes not yet stored.');
+ }
+ async function discard(){
+  if(!active||disposed)return;const s=active,e=epoch,n=write;
+  const operation=queue.then(async()=>{
+   if(!await current(e,s))return;
+   if(controller.snapshot().operationId||n!==write){publish('conflict','An unresolved save or newer edit prevents discarding this recovery copy.');return;}
+   try{
+    const expected=sequence;await options.store.discard(s,expected);if(!await current(e,s))return;
+    sequence=expected+1;known.set(localKey(s),sequence);blocked=false;
+    if(n!==write){publish('not-stored','Newer writing remains in memory while its recovery copy is stored.');return;}
+    memory.delete(localKey(s));dirty.delete(localKey(s));
+    if((controller.snapshot().canRecover||mismatched.has(localKey(s)))&&lastWorkspace){hidden.delete(localKey(s));mismatched.delete(localKey(s));await controller.bind(lastWorkspace);}
+    if(await current(e,s))publish('not-stored','Local recovery copy discarded. Current in-memory writing is unchanged.');
+   }catch(error){if(await current(e,s))failure(error);}
+  });
+  queue=operation;await operation;
+ }
+ async function recover(){
+  if(!active||disposed||!controller.snapshot().canRecover)return;const s=active,e=epoch;
+  const operation=queue.then(async()=>{
+   if(!await current(e,s)||!controller.snapshot().canRecover||mismatched.has(localKey(s)))return;
+   try{
+    const observed=await options.store.read(s);if(!await current(e,s))return;
+    if((observed?.sequence??0)!==(known.get(localKey(s))??0))throw new DraftStorageError('conflict');
+    sequence=observed?.sequence??0;blocked=false;await controller.recover();
+   }catch(error){
+    if(!await current(e,s))return;failure(error);
+    // Device failure does not revoke access to a verified memory copy. It must
+    // remain explicitly recoverable without authorizing any persistence.
+    const changedCopy=error instanceof DraftStorageError&&error.kind==='conflict';
+    if(memory.has(localKey(s))&&(!changedCopy||dirty.has(localKey(s)))&&!mismatched.has(localKey(s))&&controller.snapshot().canRecover){
+     await controller.recover();if(await current(e,s))failure(error);
+    }
+   }
+  });
+  queue=operation;await operation;
+ }
+ async function flush(){let observed:Promise<void>;do{observed=queue;await observed;}while(observed!==queue);}
+ return {controller,bind,pause,discard,recover,flush,snapshot:()=>state,subscribe(fn:()=>void){listeners.add(fn);return()=>{listeners.delete(fn);};},
+  dispose(){if(disposed)return;epoch++;active=null;controller.dispose();options.store.close();listeners.clear();memory.clear();disposed=true;}};
+}
+export type DurableManuscriptComposition=ReturnType<typeof createDurableManuscriptComposition>;

+import {document as validateDocument, saveOperation, type ManuscriptNode, type SaveOperation} from '../../../../api/src/modules/workspace/manuscript-contract';
+
+export type LocalScope = Readonly<{author:string;book:string;chapter:string;client:string}>;
+export type LocalDraft = Readonly<{schema:1;scope:LocalScope;sequence:number;timestamp:number;base:number;document:ManuscriptNode;pending:SaveOperation|null;discarded?:true}>;
+export type StorageFailure = 'unavailable'|'blocked'|'quota'|'aborted'|'corrupt'|'incompatible'|'conflict';
+export class DraftStorageError extends Error { constructor(public readonly kind:StorageFailure){super(kind);} }
+const fields=(v:object,keys:string[])=>{if(Object.keys(v).sort().join()!==keys.sort().join())throw new DraftStorageError('corrupt');};
+export function localKey(scope:LocalScope){
+ if(!scope||typeof scope!=='object')throw new DraftStorageError('corrupt');
+ fields(scope,['author','book','chapter','client']);
+ if(Object.values(scope).some(v=>typeof v!=='string'||!v||v.length>100))throw new DraftStorageError('corrupt');
+ return JSON.stringify([scope.author,scope.book,scope.chapter,scope.client]);
+}
+export function localDraft(raw:unknown,scope:LocalScope):LocalDraft {
+ try {
+  if(!raw||typeof raw!=='object'||Array.isArray(raw))throw Error();
+  const d=raw as LocalDraft;
+  if(d.schema!==1)throw new DraftStorageError('incompatible');
+  fields(d,['schema','scope','sequence','timestamp','base','document','pending',...(d.discarded===true?['discarded']:[])]);
+  if(localKey(d.scope)!==localKey(scope)||!Number.isSafeInteger(d.sequence)||d.sequence<1||!Number.isSafeInteger(d.base)||d.base<1||!Number.isSafeInteger(d.timestamp)||d.timestamp<0)throw Error();
+  const document=validateDocument(d.document),pending=d.pending===null?null:saveOperation(d.pending);
+  if(pending&&(pending.bookId!==scope.book||pending.chapterId!==scope.chapter||pending.expectedRevision>d.base))throw Error();
+  return structuredClone({...d,document,pending});
+ }catch(e){if(e instanceof DraftStorageError)throw e;throw new DraftStorageError('corrupt');}
+}
+export interface AsyncDraftStore {
+ read(scope:LocalScope):Promise<LocalDraft|null>;
+ write(scope:LocalScope,expected:number,value:Omit<LocalDraft,'sequence'>):Promise<LocalDraft>;
+ discard(scope:LocalScope,expected:number):Promise<void>;
+ close():void;
+}
+/** Native async storage. Only transaction.oncomplete is a successful write acknowledgement. */
+export function createIndexedDraftStore(factory:IDBFactory|undefined,name='vellistry-local-drafts'):AsyncDraftStore {
+ let db:IDBDatabase|null=null,closed=false,opening:Promise<IDBDatabase>|null=null;
+ const failures=(e:DOMException|null):StorageFailure=>e?.name==='QuotaExceededError'?'quota':e?.name==='AbortError'?'aborted':'unavailable';
+ function open():Promise<IDBDatabase>{
+  if(closed||!factory)return Promise.reject(new DraftStorageError('unavailable'));
+  if(db)return Promise.resolve(db);if(opening)return opening;
+  opening=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
+   let request:IDBOpenDBRequest,settled=false;
+   try{request=factory.open(name,1);}catch{reject(new DraftStorageError('unavailable'));return;}
+   request.onupgradeneeded=()=>{if(!request.result.objectStoreNames.contains('drafts'))request.result.createObjectStore('drafts');};
+   request.onblocked=()=>{settled=true;reject(new DraftStorageError('blocked'));};
+   request.onerror=()=>{settled=true;reject(new DraftStorageError(request.error?.name==='VersionError'?'incompatible':failures(request.error)));};
+   request.onsuccess=()=>{const result=request.result;if(closed||settled){result.close();if(!settled)reject(new DraftStorageError('unavailable'));settled=true;return;}db=result;db.onversionchange=()=>{result.close();db=null;opening=null;};resolve(result);};
+  });
+  return opening;
+ }
+ async function transaction<T>(mode:IDBTransactionMode,scope:LocalScope,action:(store:IDBObjectStore,key:string,finish:(v:T)=>void,fail:(e:DraftStorageError)=>void)=>void):Promise<T>{
+  const key=localKey(scope),connection=await open();if(closed)throw new DraftStorageError('unavailable');
+  return new Promise<T>((resolve,reject)=>{
+   let tx:IDBTransaction,value:T,error:DraftStorageError|undefined;
+   try{tx=connection.transaction('drafts',mode);}catch{reject(new DraftStorageError('unavailable'));return;}
+   tx.oncomplete=()=>error?reject(error):resolve(value);
+   tx.onabort=()=>reject(error??new DraftStorageError(failures(tx.error)));
+   tx.onerror=event=>{error??=new DraftStorageError(failures((event.target as IDBRequest).error??tx.error));};
+   const fail=(e:DraftStorageError)=>{error=e;tx.abort();};
+   try{action(tx.objectStore('drafts'),key,v=>{value=v;},fail);}catch(e){fail(e instanceof DraftStorageError?e:new DraftStorageError('corrupt'));}
+  });
+ }
+ return {
+  read:scope=>transaction('readonly',scope,(store,key,done,fail)=>{const r=store.get(key);r.onsuccess=()=>{try{done(r.result===undefined?null:localDraft(r.result,scope));}catch(e){fail(e as DraftStorageError);}};}),
+  write:(scope,expected,value)=>transaction('readwrite',scope,(store,key,done,fail)=>{
+   const draft=localDraft({...value,sequence:expected+1},scope),r=store.get(key);
+   r.onsuccess=()=>{try{const previous=r.result===undefined?null:localDraft(r.result,scope);if((previous?.sequence??0)!==expected)throw new DraftStorageError('conflict');store.put(draft,key);done(draft);}catch(e){fail(e as DraftStorageError);}};
+  }),
+  discard:(scope,expected)=>transaction('readwrite',scope,(store,key,done,fail)=>{const r=store.get(key);r.onsuccess=()=>{try{const previous=r.result===undefined?null:localDraft(r.result,scope);if((previous?.sequence??0)!==expected)throw new DraftStorageError('conflict');if(previous?.pending)throw new DraftStorageError('conflict');store.put({schema:1,scope,sequence:expected+1,timestamp:Date.now(),base:1,document:{type:'doc',content:[{type:'paragraph'}]},pending:null,discarded:true},key);done(undefined);}catch(e){fail(e as DraftStorageError);}};}),
+  close(){closed=true;db?.close();db=null;},
+ };
+}

import {createManuscriptComposition,type ManuscriptTransport} from './manuscript-composition';
import {type AsyncDraftStore,type LocalScope,localKey,DraftStorageError} from './manuscript-indexeddb';
import {saveOperation} from '../../../../api/src/modules/workspace/manuscript-contract';
import type {WorkspaceSnapshot} from '../workspace/book-workspace-session';

export type LocalPhase='not-stored'|'storing'|'stored'|'unavailable'|'blocked'|'quota'|'aborted'|'corrupt'|'incompatible'|'conflict'|'recovery'|'unverified';
/** Test-only composition. The existing recovery map is memory; IndexedDB is a separate async port. */
export function createDurableManuscriptComposition(options:{readOwner:()=>Promise<string|null>;transport:ManuscriptTransport;store:AsyncDraftStore;clientId:string;nextOperationId:()=>string;now?:()=>number}){
 let epoch=0,disposed=false,active:LocalScope|null=null,sequence=0,write=0,blocked=false;
 let lastWorkspace:WorkspaceSnapshot|null=null;let queue=Promise.resolve();const memory=new Map<string,unknown>(),hidden=new Set<string>(),mismatched=new Set<string>(),known=new Map<string,number>(),dirty=new Set<string>(),listeners=new Set<()=>void>();
 let state:Readonly<{phase:LocalPhase;message:string}>=Object.freeze({phase:'unverified' as LocalPhase,message:'Identity unavailable. Local recovery is concealed.'});
 function publish(phase:LocalPhase,message:string){if(disposed)return;state=Object.freeze({phase,message});listeners.forEach(f=>f());}
 async function current(e:number,s:LocalScope){if(disposed||epoch!==e||!active||localKey(active)!==localKey(s))return false;let owner:string|null;try{owner=await options.readOwner();}catch{owner=null;}if(disposed||epoch!==e)return false;if(owner!==s.author){pause();return false;}return true;}
 function failure(e:unknown){const kind=e instanceof DraftStorageError?e.kind:'unavailable';blocked=true;publish(kind,kind==='conflict'?'Another tab changed this recovery copy. Your writing remains in memory; choose the copy explicitly.':'Unable to store a recovery copy. Your current writing remains in memory.');}
 function schedule(raw:unknown){
  if(!active||disposed)return;const s=active,e=epoch,n=++write;dirty.add(localKey(active));if(blocked)return;
  // Clone at dispatch, before any asynchronous work or later editor mutation.
  const d=structuredClone(raw) as {text:string;base:number;pending?:{id:string;text:string;base:number}};
  publish('storing','Storing a recovery copy on this device...');
  queue=queue.then(async()=>{
   if(!await current(e,s)||blocked)return;
   try{
    const pending=d.pending?saveOperation({operationId:d.pending.id,bookId:s.book,chapterId:s.chapter,expectedRevision:d.pending.base,document:JSON.parse(d.pending.text)}):null;
    const result=await options.store.write(s,sequence,{schema:1,scope:s,timestamp:(options.now??Date.now)(),base:d.base,document:JSON.parse(d.text),pending});
    known.set(localKey(s),result.sequence);if(JSON.stringify(memory.get(localKey(s)))===JSON.stringify(d))dirty.delete(localKey(s));if(!await current(e,s))return;sequence=result.sequence;
    if(n===write)publish('stored','Saved on this device, not synced.');
   }catch(error){if(await current(e,s))failure(error);}
  });
 }
 const transport:ManuscriptTransport={...options.transport,async save(s,operation){const e=epoch,scope=active;if(!scope||!await current(e,scope))throw Error('Identity unavailable');await queue;if(!await current(e,scope)||blocked)throw Error('Pending operation not stored');const durable=await options.store.read(scope);if(!await current(e,scope)||!durable?.pending||JSON.stringify(durable.pending)!==JSON.stringify(operation))throw Error('Pending operation not stored');return options.transport.save(s,operation);},async read(s){
  const readEpoch=epoch,readScope=active;
  const result=await options.transport.read(s) as {status:string;value?:{revision:number;document:unknown}};
  if(readScope&&await current(readEpoch,readScope)&&active&&s.author===active.author&&s.bookId===active.book&&s.chapterId===active.chapter){
   const k=localKey(active),entry=memory.get(k) as {base:number;text:string;pending?:{base:number;text:string}}|undefined;
   if(entry&&result.status==='ready'&&result.value){
    const accepted=result.value;
    const ackLost=entry.pending&&accepted.revision===entry.pending.base+1&&JSON.stringify(accepted.document)===entry.pending.text;
    if(entry.base!==accepted.revision&&!ackLost){hidden.add(k);mismatched.add(k);blocked=true;publish('conflict','A recovery copy has a different accepted revision. It was retained without replacing accepted text.');}
    else if(!entry.pending&&entry.text===JSON.stringify(accepted.document))hidden.add(k);
   }
  }
  return result;
 }};
 const controller=createManuscriptComposition({...options,transport,storage:{get:k=>hidden.has(k)?undefined:memory.get(k),set(k,v){hidden.delete(k);memory.set(k,v);schedule(v);}}});
 // Existing memory key ordering is [author,book,chapter,client], identical to localKey.
 function pause(){epoch++;active=null;blocked=true;controller.pause();publish('unverified','Identity unavailable. Local recovery is concealed.');}
 async function bind(workspace:WorkspaceSnapshot){
  lastWorkspace=workspace;const e=++epoch;active=null;blocked=true;controller.pause();publish('unverified','Checking identity and accepted revision before local recovery...');
  let owner:string|null;try{owner=await options.readOwner();}catch{owner=null;}
  if(disposed||e!==epoch)return;
  if(!owner||!workspace.document||!workspace.selectedChapterId){pause();return;}
  const s=Object.freeze({author:owner,book:workspace.document.bookId,chapter:workspace.selectedChapterId,client:options.clientId});
  active=s;sequence=0;write=0;blocked=false;hidden.delete(localKey(s));mismatched.delete(localKey(s));
  try{
   const candidate=await options.store.read(s);if(!await current(e,s))return;
   sequence=candidate?.sequence??0;const k=localKey(s);if(!known.has(k))known.set(k,sequence);if(memory.has(k)&&dirty.has(k)){if(known.has(k)&&known.get(k)!==sequence){failure(new DraftStorageError('conflict'));}else{blocked=true;publish('not-stored','A newer in-memory recovery copy is available. It has not been stored.');}}else{known.set(k,sequence);memory.delete(k);if(candidate&&!candidate.discarded)memory.set(localKey(s),{scope:{author:s.author,book:s.book,chapter:s.chapter},text:JSON.stringify(candidate.document),base:candidate.base,phase:'recoverable',...(candidate.pending?{pending:{id:candidate.pending.operationId,text:JSON.stringify(candidate.pending.document),base:candidate.pending.expectedRevision}}:{})});}
  }catch(error){if(!await current(e,s))return;failure(error);}
  if(!await current(e,s))return;await controller.bind(workspace);if(!await current(e,s))return;if(mismatched.has(localKey(s)))controller.editorSession.snapshot().editor?.setEditable(false,false);
  if(!blocked)publish(controller.snapshot().canRecover?'recovery':'not-stored',controller.snapshot().canRecover?'Recovery copy available. Choose explicitly; accepted text is unchanged.':'Local changes not yet stored.');
 }
 async function discard(){
  if(!active||disposed)return;const s=active,e=epoch,n=write;
  const operation=queue.then(async()=>{
   if(!await current(e,s))return;
   if(controller.snapshot().operationId||n!==write){publish('conflict','An unresolved save or newer edit prevents discarding this recovery copy.');return;}
   try{
    const expected=sequence;await options.store.discard(s,expected);if(!await current(e,s))return;
    sequence=expected+1;known.set(localKey(s),sequence);blocked=false;
    if(n!==write){publish('not-stored','Newer writing remains in memory while its recovery copy is stored.');return;}
    memory.delete(localKey(s));dirty.delete(localKey(s));
    if((controller.snapshot().canRecover||mismatched.has(localKey(s)))&&lastWorkspace){hidden.delete(localKey(s));mismatched.delete(localKey(s));await controller.bind(lastWorkspace);}
    if(await current(e,s))publish('not-stored','Local recovery copy discarded. Current in-memory writing is unchanged.');
   }catch(error){if(await current(e,s))failure(error);}
  });
  queue=operation;await operation;
 }
 async function recover(){
  if(!active||disposed||!controller.snapshot().canRecover)return;const s=active,e=epoch;
  const operation=queue.then(async()=>{
   if(!await current(e,s)||!controller.snapshot().canRecover||mismatched.has(localKey(s)))return;
   try{
    const observed=await options.store.read(s);if(!await current(e,s))return;
    if((observed?.sequence??0)!==(known.get(localKey(s))??0))throw new DraftStorageError('conflict');
    sequence=observed?.sequence??0;blocked=false;await controller.recover();
   }catch(error){
    if(!await current(e,s))return;failure(error);
    // Device failure does not revoke access to a verified memory copy. It must
    // remain explicitly recoverable without authorizing any persistence.
    const changedCopy=error instanceof DraftStorageError&&error.kind==='conflict';
    if(memory.has(localKey(s))&&(!changedCopy||dirty.has(localKey(s)))&&!mismatched.has(localKey(s))&&controller.snapshot().canRecover){
     await controller.recover();if(await current(e,s))failure(error);
    }
   }
  });
  queue=operation;await operation;
 }
 async function flush(){let observed:Promise<void>;do{observed=queue;await observed;}while(observed!==queue);}
 return {controller,bind,pause,discard,recover,flush,snapshot:()=>state,subscribe(fn:()=>void){listeners.add(fn);return()=>{listeners.delete(fn);};},
  dispose(){if(disposed)return;epoch++;active=null;controller.dispose();options.store.close();listeners.clear();memory.clear();disposed=true;}};
}

import {document as validateDocument, saveOperation, type ManuscriptNode, type SaveOperation} from '../../../../api/src/modules/workspace/manuscript-contract';

export type LocalScope = Readonly<{author:string;book:string;chapter:string;client:string}>;
export type LocalDraft = Readonly<{schema:1;scope:LocalScope;sequence:number;timestamp:number;base:number;document:ManuscriptNode;pending:SaveOperation|null;discarded?:true}>;
export type StorageFailure = 'unavailable'|'blocked'|'quota'|'aborted'|'corrupt'|'incompatible'|'conflict';
export class DraftStorageError extends Error { constructor(public readonly kind:StorageFailure){super(kind);} }
const fields=(v:object,keys:string[])=>{if(Object.keys(v).sort().join()!==keys.sort().join())throw new DraftStorageError('corrupt');};
export function localKey(scope:LocalScope){
 if(!scope||typeof scope!=='object')throw new DraftStorageError('corrupt');
 fields(scope,['author','book','chapter','client']);
 if(Object.values(scope).some(v=>typeof v!=='string'||!v||v.length>100))throw new DraftStorageError('corrupt');
 return JSON.stringify([scope.author,scope.book,scope.chapter,scope.client]);
}
export function localDraft(raw:unknown,scope:LocalScope):LocalDraft {
 try {
  if(!raw||typeof raw!=='object'||Array.isArray(raw))throw Error();
  const d=raw as LocalDraft;
  if(d.schema!==1)throw new DraftStorageError('incompatible');
  fields(d,['schema','scope','sequence','timestamp','base','document','pending',...(d.discarded===true?['discarded']:[])]);
  if(localKey(d.scope)!==localKey(scope)||!Number.isSafeInteger(d.sequence)||d.sequence<1||!Number.isSafeInteger(d.base)||d.base<1||!Number.isSafeInteger(d.timestamp)||d.timestamp<0)throw Error();
  const document=validateDocument(d.document),pending=d.pending===null?null:saveOperation(d.pending);
  if(pending&&(pending.bookId!==scope.book||pending.chapterId!==scope.chapter||pending.expectedRevision>d.base))throw Error();
  return structuredClone({...d,document,pending});
 }catch(e){if(e instanceof DraftStorageError)throw e;throw new DraftStorageError('corrupt');}
}
export interface AsyncDraftStore {
 read(scope:LocalScope):Promise<LocalDraft|null>;
 write(scope:LocalScope,expected:number,value:Omit<LocalDraft,'sequence'>):Promise<LocalDraft>;
 discard(scope:LocalScope,expected:number):Promise<void>;
 close():void;
}
/** Native async storage. Only transaction.oncomplete is a successful write acknowledgement. */
export function createIndexedDraftStore(factory:IDBFactory|undefined,name='vellistry-local-drafts'):AsyncDraftStore {
 let db:IDBDatabase|null=null,closed=false,opening:Promise<IDBDatabase>|null=null;
 const failures=(e:DOMException|null):StorageFailure=>e?.name==='QuotaExceededError'?'quota':e?.name==='AbortError'?'aborted':'unavailable';
 function open():Promise<IDBDatabase>{
  if(closed||!factory)return Promise.reject(new DraftStorageError('unavailable'));
  if(db)return Promise.resolve(db);if(opening)return opening;
  opening=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
   let request:IDBOpenDBRequest,settled=false;
   try{request=factory.open(name,1);}catch{reject(new DraftStorageError('unavailable'));return;}
   request.onupgradeneeded=()=>{if(!request.result.objectStoreNames.contains('drafts'))request.result.createObjectStore('drafts');};
   request.onblocked=()=>{settled=true;reject(new DraftStorageError('blocked'));};
   request.onerror=()=>{settled=true;reject(new DraftStorageError(request.error?.name==='VersionError'?'incompatible':failures(request.error)));};
   request.onsuccess=()=>{const result=request.result;if(closed||settled){result.close();if(!settled)reject(new DraftStorageError('unavailable'));settled=true;return;}db=result;db.onversionchange=()=>{result.close();db=null;opening=null;};resolve(result);};
  });
  return opening;
 }
 async function transaction<T>(mode:IDBTransactionMode,scope:LocalScope,action:(store:IDBObjectStore,key:string,finish:(v:T)=>void,fail:(e:DraftStorageError)=>void)=>void):Promise<T>{
  const key=localKey(scope),connection=await open();if(closed)throw new DraftStorageError('unavailable');
  return new Promise<T>((resolve,reject)=>{
   let tx:IDBTransaction,value:T,error:DraftStorageError|undefined;
   try{tx=connection.transaction('drafts',mode);}catch{reject(new DraftStorageError('unavailable'));return;}
   tx.oncomplete=()=>error?reject(error):resolve(value);
   tx.onabort=()=>reject(error??new DraftStorageError(failures(tx.error)));
   tx.onerror=event=>{error??=new DraftStorageError(failures((event.target as IDBRequest).error??tx.error));};
   const fail=(e:DraftStorageError)=>{error=e;tx.abort();};
   try{action(tx.objectStore('drafts'),key,v=>{value=v;},fail);}catch(e){fail(e instanceof DraftStorageError?e:new DraftStorageError('corrupt'));}
  });
 }
 return {
  read:scope=>transaction('readonly',scope,(store,key,done,fail)=>{const r=store.get(key);r.onsuccess=()=>{try{done(r.result===undefined?null:localDraft(r.result,scope));}catch(e){fail(e as DraftStorageError);}};}),
  write:(scope,expected,value)=>transaction('readwrite',scope,(store,key,done,fail)=>{
   const draft=localDraft({...value,sequence:expected+1},scope),r=store.get(key);
   r.onsuccess=()=>{try{const previous=r.result===undefined?null:localDraft(r.result,scope);if((previous?.sequence??0)!==expected)throw new DraftStorageError('conflict');store.put(draft,key);done(draft);}catch(e){fail(e as DraftStorageError);}};
  }),
  discard:(scope,expected)=>transaction('readwrite',scope,(store,key,done,fail)=>{const r=store.get(key);r.onsuccess=()=>{try{const previous=r.result===undefined?null:localDraft(r.result,scope);if((previous?.sequence??0)!==expected)throw new DraftStorageError('conflict');if(previous?.pending)throw new DraftStorageError('conflict');store.put({schema:1,scope,sequence:expected+1,timestamp:Date.now(),base:1,document:{type:'doc',content:[{type:'paragraph'}]},pending:null,discarded:true},key);done(undefined);}catch(e){fail(e as DraftStorageError);}};}),
  close(){closed=true;db?.close();db=null;},
 };
}

/** Deterministic synthetic recovery. Storage is injected; no browser/device durability claim. */
export type DraftScope=Readonly<{author:string;book:string;chapter:string}>;
export type DraftPhase="unsaved"|"saving"|"confirmed"|"rejected"|"conflicted"|"unknown"|"recoverable"|"unverified";
export type DraftStorage={get(key:string):unknown;set(key:string,value:unknown):void};
type Pending={id:string;text:string;base:number};
type Entry={scope:DraftScope;text:string;base:number;phase:DraftPhase;pending?:Pending};
export function createDraftRecovery(storage:DraftStorage,validate:(value:unknown)=>unknown,clientId:string){
 if(typeof clientId!=="string"||!clientId||clientId.length>100)throw new Error("Explicit synthetic client identity required");
 let active:Entry|null=null;let epoch=0;
 const key=(s:DraftScope)=>JSON.stringify([s.author,s.book,s.chapter,clientId]);
 function checked(s:DraftScope){if(!s||Object.keys(s).sort().join()!=="author,book,chapter"||Object.values(s).some(v=>typeof v!=="string"||!v||v.length>100))throw new Error("Invalid scope");return Object.freeze({...s});}
 function persist(){if(active)storage.set(key(active.scope),JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(active)));}
 function snapshot(){return active?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(active)) as Entry:null;}
 return {
  snapshot,
  pendingTicket(){return active?.pending?Object.freeze({epoch,scope:active.scope,id:active.pending.id}):null;},
  bind(scope:DraftScope|null,accepted:unknown,revision:number){epoch++;active=null;if(!scope)return;const s=checked(scope);if(!Number.isSafeInteger(revision)||revision<1)throw new Error("Invalid revision");const text=JSON.stringify(validate(accepted));active={scope:s,text,base:revision,phase:"confirmed"};},
  pause(){epoch++;if(active)active.phase="unverified";active=null;},
  edit(value:unknown){if(!active)throw new Error("Unverified identity");active.text=JSON.stringify(validate(value));active.phase=active.pending?"unknown":"unsaved";persist();},
  begin(id:string){if(!active||active.pending||active.phase!=="unsaved"||typeof id!=="string"||!id||id.length>100)throw new Error("Save unavailable");active.pending={id,text:active.text,base:active.base};active.phase="saving";persist();return Object.freeze({epoch,scope:active.scope,...active.pending});},
  settle(ticket:{epoch:number;scope:DraftScope;id:string},outcome:"confirmed"|"rejected"|"conflicted"|"unknown",revision?:number){
   if(!active||ticket.epoch!==epoch||key(ticket.scope)!==key(active.scope)||active.pending?.id!==ticket.id)return false;
   const pending=active.pending;
   if(outcome==="confirmed"&&revision===pending.base+1){const stale=revision<active.base;active.base=Math.max(active.base,revision);active.phase=stale?"conflicted":active.text===pending.text?"confirmed":"unsaved";delete active.pending;}
   else if(outcome==="rejected"||outcome==="conflicted"){active.phase=outcome;delete active.pending;}
   else active.phase="unknown";persist();return true;
  },
  recovery(){if(!active)return null;try{const candidate=storage.get(key(active.scope)) as Entry;if(!candidate||key(checked(candidate.scope))!==key(active.scope)||!Number.isSafeInteger(candidate.base)||candidate.base<1||typeof candidate.text!=="string"||candidate.text.length>300000)return null;validate(JSON.parse(candidate.text));if(candidate.pending){if(typeof candidate.pending.id!=="string"||!candidate.pending.id||candidate.pending.id.length>100||(!Number.isSafeInteger(candidate.pending.base)||candidate.pending.base<1||candidate.pending.base>candidate.base)) return null;validate(JSON.parse(candidate.pending.text));}return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify({...candidate,phase:"recoverable"}));}catch{return null;}},
  recover(candidate:unknown){if(!active)throw new Error("Identity unavailable");const actual=this.recovery();if(!actual||JSON.stringify(actual)!==JSON.stringify(candidate))throw new Error("Invalid recovery candidate");active.text=actual.text;active.pending=actual.pending;active.phase=actual.pending?"unknown":"unsaved";/* retain current accepted base; never auto-save recovered content */persist();},
 };
}

# LANGUAGE.md - Vellistry product language and terminology

> **Adoption status:** Complete, implementation-ready **proposed language contract**, not an already approved owner decision. On explicit owner approval and repository integration, the `APPROVED_CONCEPT` terms below express already approved product meaning, and owner-approved `PROPOSED_LABEL` entries become the effective UI vocabulary. Before approval, do not retroactively relabel proposals as accepted or silently update application code. The file may guide reviews as a candidate immediately, but its presence alone does not change product scope, technical contracts, or owner authorization.

## 0. Quick reference for product designers, writers, and coding agents

1. **The author is in control.** Do not imply the application or AI independently authors, accepts, publishes, or establishes story truth.
2. **Use familiar writing words.** Prefer **book**, **chapter**, **manuscript**, **character**, **story thread**, and **writing rule** in general explanations; reserve **Story Profiles** and **Rules Studio** for the named Vellistry surfaces.
3. **Never collapse these boundaries:** manuscript / outline / notes; draft / saved revision; AI suggestion / accepted prose; suggested fact / approved story fact; AI prose acceptance / fact approval; local recovery / confirmed online save; exported document / full book backup.
4. **Actions name their effect:** **Create book**, **Add chapter**, **Continue writing**, **Rewrite selection**, **Accept suggestion**, **Reject suggestion**, **Approve fact**, **Dismiss suggestion**, **Download manuscript**, **Download book backup**, **Sign out**. Display-name choices identified as `PROPOSED_LABEL` need owner review before deployment.
5. **Status is evidence, not decoration.** Never display **Saved** without a server durability acknowledgement or **Saved on this device** without confirmed local persistence. A lost acknowledgement is **Save status unknown**, not necessarily **Save failed** or **Conflict**.
6. **AI is optional.** Never require an AI key for ordinary book writing, manual Story Profiles, rule editing, or supported export. Do not imply AI is free or that Vellistry pays provider bills.
7. **Current shipping boundary:** live implementation is login and a basic book library. Described chapter/editor/rules/AI UI is mainly approved product scope plus proposed UX, not verified deployed behavior.
8. **No silent renaming of technology:** labels here do not authorize changes to routes, SQL columns, RPCs, auth tokens, IDs, model/provider routing, source guidance files, or migration history.
9. **On an undefined or disputed term:** reuse a known semantic term where it fits exactly; otherwise add a proposed entry and request the owner's decision. Never invent another competing name.
10. **Check the exact affected screen and state** before using an example from this document. Example copy is a template conditioned on the specified behavior, never evidence that a feature works.

## 1. Purpose, authority, and applicability

### 1.1 What this file governs

This standard covers visible navigation, feature and page names, menus, breadcrumbs, tab names, buttons, form fields, helper text, tooltips, search and filters, confirmation dialogs, errors, empty states, saving and AI progress, notices, accessibility names, onboarding, author-visible revision provenance, export and privacy language, and future localized content. It also defines the semantic boundaries agents must preserve when writing content.

It **does not** standardize TypeScript/Python style, rename internal API or database artifacts, choose visual styling, decide new backend behaviors, authorize subscriptions, certify third-party rights, or approve extra MVP features. UI wording must follow tested behavior and accessibility requirements.

### 1.2 Authority and precedence

Apply authority in this order:

1. Explicitly owner-approved product scope, owner decisions, `PROJECT-CONSTITUTION.md`, accepted ADRs and security/privacy boundaries.
2. Verified code, real integration results and actual enabled features for truthful behavioral claims. Code does not automatically overrule the approved product goal; discrepancies are recorded as implementation gaps.
3. Once approved: **this document** for visible words that do not conflict with levels 1-2; source-of-truth change control applies.
4. Dated user-journey and design specifications (`VEL-JRN-001..011`, PLAN-03, editable Figma master) where not superseded; they are not all accepted routes/copy.
5. Competitor terminology, style guides and analyst proposals as supporting research, not independent authority.

Consult `CURRENT-STATE.md` and live GitHub status before claiming something is integrated or complete. Do not treat a proposed Figma route, a synthetic demonstration, or a historical PR as a production capability. The exact repository snapshot used to prepare this candidate is `f371448` on 2026-10-16; re-audit when adopting.

### 1.3 Status codes for this vocabulary

| Code | Interpretation | Implementation rule |
|---|---|---|
| `EXISTING_UI` | Exact or near-exact string verified in `main` application code | Preserve unless independently reviewed; it may still need refinement |
| `APPROVED_CONCEPT` | The named behavior/concept is supported by owner-approved scope, ADR, or later decision | Preserve meaning; suggested exact title-case/display capitalization may still be proposed |
| `PROPOSED_LABEL` | Recommended visible wording informed by evidence | Candidate to adopt; **not** pre-approved merely because listed here |
| `FUTURE_RESERVED` | Useful name for an explicitly deferred feature, not an MVP menu item | No enabled UI, marketing claim, or new scope without separate approval |
| `PENDING_DECISION` | Name or semantic mapping unresolved or dependent on unverified behavior | Do not enforce as final or show as enabled without review |
| `INTERNAL_ONLY` | Engineering/process name, not a suitable general UI label | May remain unchanged in code, data, logs and engineering docs |

Use `status` **per term** and, for high-impact changes, **per specific UI label**. A product concept's approval does not make every draft string official.

## 2. Product context and audiences

**Product:** Vellistry, an author-controlled fiction writing and editing application. The approved first release is a manual-first writing environment with optional scoped AI suggestions, editable character/story-thread profiles, configurable fiction guidance and durable/portable manuscript handling. Later opportunities include series-level structure, deep story intelligence, collaboration, covers and professional publication output; these are **not** initial MVP features.

**Primary role:** **Author** - a signed-in person who owns and edits their own books, rules and approved story information. In user-facing first-person/system copy prefer **you**, **your books**, **your manuscript** over repeatedly saying "the author." The existing system has one relevant product role. There is no approved collaborator, editor, publisher, staff, moderator or administrator role model for the MVP. Do not invent corresponding role switchers or access claims.

**Intended writers:** novelists, serialized/web-fiction writers, and writers in many genres. Expect users who know ordinary concepts such as books, chapters, drafts and characters, not necessarily APIs, tokens, source revisions, database row-level security or model context-window mechanics. Some writers avoid AI deliberately; this is a supported experience, not an onboarding failure.

**Experience constraints:** desktop-first authoring with responsive consideration; keyboard, screen reader, IME and RTL behavior require actual testing. Product English is the current source language (`<html lang="en">` in `apps/web/src/app/layout.tsx`); no locale rollout or Ghana-only formatting has been approved. Treat region, spelling preferences and date/currency format as localization decisions, not identity-based assumptions.

## 3. Fundamental language principles

1. **Describe the author's task, not an implementation artifact.** "Review changes" rather than "Resolve CAS conflict"; "Your books" rather than "books table".
2. **Give one concept one primary name.** Shorter contextual labels are permitted if they cannot change the concept: **Rules Studio** (navigation)  **Rules** (short tab inside that studio), never "Prompts" for the same object.
3. **Do not merge neighboring concepts.** Book vs series; chapter vs scene; notes vs manuscript; outline vs accepted prose; rule vs canon; suggestion vs revision; connection vs selected model; download vs backup.
4. **Use specific action verbs.** "Create book" rather than "Create" outside an obvious create-book form. "Approve fact" rather than "Accept" when the choice changes approved story information.
5. **Show provenance and degree of certainty.** A proposed fact is **Suggested fact**, not **Fact added**; a saved draft needs an actual receipt.
6. **Preserve creative agency and style.** Language should explain choices, not score an author's prose as objectively "good/bad" or claim to detect AI-written text.
7. **Do not blame authors.** State the problem and practical recovery action; never imply a session/token/storage failure is their fault.
8. **No false reassurance.** Never say *permanently saved*, *fully secure*, *guaranteed original*, *always private*, *completely offline*, *lossless DOCX*, *zero cost*, or *immediately revoked* without specific verified guarantees.
9. **Don't promise availability.** An approved/future feature is not an active control until implemented, tested and authorized for its environment.
10. **Write for accessible interaction.** A meaningful visible label and matching accessible name must work without relying only on color, icon, placeholder, tooltip or pointer location.
11. **Use progressively disclosed detail.** Short labels for ordinary actions; precise explanatory copy for revisions, consent, data loss and security boundaries.
12. **Preserve author-supplied wording.** Names of books, chapters, characters, titles, custom rules and manuscript prose are the author's data, not strings agents should normalize automatically.

## 4. Canonical vocabulary and concept boundaries

**Reading the table:** `Primary UI term` is current where `EXISTING_UI`, describes an accepted *concept* where `APPROVED_CONCEPT`, and is a recommended display word requiring owner adoption where `PROPOSED_LABEL`. Entries can carry more than one category. Do not infer that a not-yet-built object has a live screen.

### 4.1 Authoring, documents and manuscript structure

| Primary UI term | Meaning and correct scope | Contextual alternative | Avoid / do not confuse with | Basis / status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Vellistry** | Working product name used in current UI/repo | None | Generic "AI writer" as sole product name | `EXISTING_UI`; brand/trademark clearance unresolved |
| **Your books** | Signed-in author's book collection, existing page heading `/books` | **Books** in compact navigation; **Book library** in help text | Projects or dashboard when no project/dashboard entity exists | `EXISTING_UI` |
| **Book** | Author-owned manuscript container; an independently organized work | Novel in explanatory prose if genre appropriate | Project, series, document, chapter | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
| **Book title** | Name of one book, currently required 1-120 characters | Title within its book form | New book title as permanent field label, document name | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Chapter** | Ordered component of a book; chapter identity independent of display order | Chapter number in contextual metadata | Scene; each may later be separate | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; chapter UI not implemented |
| **Chapter title** | Author's label for a chapter | Title inside clearly scoped chapter form | Heading style, book title | `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Manuscript** | Accepted prose organized into chapters; the text intended as the book | **Writing** as an action/short workspace heading | Outline, planning note, generated alternative, published edition | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
| **Manuscript editor** | The place to write and format the book without AI | **Editor** when surrounding context is clear | AI Studio, chat, layout/print editor | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
| **Writing workspace** | Work area combining manuscript and adjacent author tools | **Book workspace** for book-level shell | "Document management console" | `PROPOSED_LABEL`; route/layout proposed |
| **Planning notes** | Non-manuscript notes supporting a chapter/book | **Notes** inside scoped view | Canon, scene prose, revisions | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; exact nav `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Outline** | Planned structure or future events; can differ from what has happened | Story plan, only explanatory | Current story facts, manuscript | `FUTURE_RESERVED` as dedicated MVP module; concept distinct |
| **Scene** | A bounded narrative event/passage inside a chapter | Scene summary as a planning record | Chapter; scene-level route/schema is not approved for MVP | `FUTURE_RESERVED` / `PENDING_DECISION` |
| **Series** | Grouping of books and shared narrative continuity | Book series | Book or workspace; not a present first-MVP object | `FUTURE_RESERVED` |
| **Word count** | Number of words using the implementation's defined counting behavior | `1,234 words` as displayed value | Estimated pages or reading time | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; counting rules to test |
| **Focus mode** | Reduced-distraction manual writing view | Distraction-free writing in help | Full-screen alone, read-only preview | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; exact UI `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Revision** | Persisted accepted version of a chapter/manuscript | **Version** in simpler explanations, if clearly a saved snapshot | Unsaved browser draft; AI suggestion; backup | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
| **Revision history** | Author-visible record of saved accepted revisions, subject to implemented retention | **History** within manuscript only | Browser Undo stack; generated proposal list | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Recovered draft** | Local/recoverable writing not automatically accepted as newest server revision | Local draft if provenance requires it | Restored accepted revision; approved story fact | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; `PROPOSED_LABEL` |

### 4.2 Story knowledge and author approval

| Primary UI term | Meaning and correct scope | Contextual alternative | Avoid / do not confuse with | Basis / status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Story Profiles** | Named MVP capability for editable character/storyline information and approved evolving state | **Profiles** within Story Workspace | Story Bible as an automatic synonym for every profile | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
| **Story Workspace** | Proposed larger view housing profiles, threads and future supporting material | **Story** only if navigation context makes it clear | Treating a proposed view as an existing route | `PROPOSED_LABEL`; Figma/design terminology |
| **Character** | Person/being within a given book, with identity and changing state | Character profile when viewing its record | User account, role, actor, narrator | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
| **Character profile** | Author-maintained facts and evolving state for one character | Character in a clearly titled Characters view | Full accepted manuscript, AI character draft | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
| **Story thread** | Continuing plotline, obligation or subplot whose development may change | **Thread** in its own list | Chat thread, discussion thread; unrelated issue tracker | `PROPOSED_LABEL` based on approved Storyline/Thread concept |
| **Storyline** | Descriptive prose synonym for plot development | Story thread only when describing same supported record | Automatic alias for exact `thread` data record | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` semantically; nav mapping `PENDING_DECISION` |
| **Suggested fact** | Proposed new/changed character or story information awaiting review | **Suggested change** in a tightly scoped review panel | Approved fact, canon, verified truth | `PROPOSED_LABEL` for approved candidate workflow |
| **Approved fact** | Author-approved, appropriately sourced story information used for supported continuity context | **Story fact** when already known approved | Extracted claim, AI guess, character belief | `PROPOSED_LABEL` for approved state distinction |
| **Story canon** | Accepted set of relevant established fictional facts and world rules | **Canon** after explanation | Entire Story Bible; all text in manuscript; narrator's unreliable assertion | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; use sparingly for general audience |
| **Source passage** | Exact passage/evidence associated with a proposed fact | **Source** if action context is obvious | AI-generated text that was rejected; undocumented claim | `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Story time** | When something occurs in the fictional chronology | **When it happens** in help | Chapter order/publication order/time UI displays | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; advanced chronology beyond MVP limited |
| **Character knowledge** | Information a character could plausibly know at a specified story point | **What [name] knows** in help | Reader knowledge, global source facts, narrator knowledge | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; full automated POV firewall deferred |
| **Needs review** | A candidate/approved fact requires author attention because evidence changed or is uncertain | Review needed in compact list | Rejected, false, or automatically invalidated | `PROPOSED_LABEL`; proof/evidence-driven only |
| **Retcon** | Deliberate retrospective change to established fiction | **Change to earlier story events** at first encounter | Error, automatic correction | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; explain term for new authors |
| **Story Bible** | Broad, established genre term for character/world/plot reference material | In explanatory content about the wider concept | Renaming Live Story Profiles or Story Workspace to it without approval | `FUTURE_RESERVED` / `PENDING_DECISION` as Vellistry feature name |

### 4.3 Rules, guidance and AI

| Primary UI term | Meaning and correct scope | Contextual alternative | Avoid / do not confuse with | Basis / status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Rules Studio** | Named author-facing feature for default/custom guidance and scope controls | **Rules** in a tab within the feature | Prompt library, policy engine, AI Studio | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
| **Writing rule** | A configurable creative instruction/guideline | **Rule** when referring to a row | Canon fact; security policy; absolute literary prohibition | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Default rule** | Historical guidance supplied by Vellistry via owner-approved certified source mapping | Included rule in help | Arbitrarily rewritten 130-lens content | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; package/mapping unresolved |
| **Custom rule** | Guidance created by this author | Your rule | Mutated source default | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
| **Rule scope** | Where a rule applies, e.g. workspace/book in initial bounded plan | **Applies to** in field labels | Permission scope/API scope | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Rule exception** | Visible author preference that deliberately qualifies inherited guidance | **Exception** | Hidden override or generic algorithmic exemption | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
| **Enabled / Disabled** | Whether a specific rule is included in applicable effective guidance | On/Off in compact controls with accessible names | Approved/rejected fact; stored/deleted rule | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
| **Applied guidance** | Applicable enabled subset for the current writing request | **What the AI will receive** if preview shows complete outbound context | Proof the model obeyed the guidance | `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **AI assistance** | Optional model-assisted suggestions within writing workflow | **AI** in concise navigation where clarity survives | Mandatory authoring engine | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
| **AI connection** | Configured access route using an author's API credential | Connect AI in action copy | Model itself; permanently saved key | `PROPOSED_LABEL` for approved BYOK behavior |
| **AI provider** | External service handling model requests, initially selected OpenRouter route | Provider in advanced settings | Vellistry itself as model trainer | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
| **Model** | Selected downstream model available via authorized provider route | AI model when first introduced | Provider or API key | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
| **API key** | Author-supplied secret used to authorize optional AI requests | Your API key | Public account password; Vellistry paid credits | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; technical term unavoidable at setup |
| **Continue writing** | AI proposes plain text at valid author-selected collapsed caret | **Continue** in an editor menu with known context | Add to chapter end; automatic writing | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; display form `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Rewrite selection** | AI proposes alternate text for one contiguous selected span within one paragraph | **Rewrite** when one span is already selected | Rewrite entire chapter/book; accepting immediately | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; display form `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **AI suggestion** | Unaccepted model-proposed alternative, held separately from saved prose | **Suggestion** | Saved revision; direct accepted text; confirmed fact | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Review suggestion** | Compare proposed prose against the source and choose a disposition | Review | Apply automatically | `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Accept suggestion** | Author decision to commit reviewed AI prose, subject to revision validation | **Accept** inside unambiguous suggestion card | Approve fact; silently replace source | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; display form `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Reject suggestion** | Discard AI proposed prose without changing accepted document | **Reject** inside suggestion card | Delete manuscript; dismiss fact | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; display form `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Approve fact** | Separate author decision making a suggested story fact authoritative | **Approve** in clearly fact-only view | Accept AI prose | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; display form `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Dismiss suggestion** | Decline a candidate fact/change without establishing it | **Dismiss** in fact-review context | Delete already approved fact, reject prose | `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Undo accepted change** | Compensating revision to reverse eligible last accepted proposal | **Undo** where affordance is clear | Delete arbitrary later edits | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Outdated suggestion** | Proposal pinned to earlier text/selection and no longer safe to accept | **Needs a new suggestion** in recovery CTA | Save conflict, rule disabled | `PROPOSED_LABEL` |

### 4.4 Portability, privacy and account

| Primary UI term | Meaning and scope | Avoid / distinction | Basis / status |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Import manuscript** | Bring supported external text/documents into an author's book after fidelity checks | Never equate with clean full-backup restore | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Download manuscript** | Export a book/chapter into supported ordinary file format (TXT/DOCX) | Not an independent full-application backup or publication master | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **File format** | The kind of downloaded/imported document (TXT/DOCX) | Publishing layout specification | `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Download book backup** | Export versioned portable archive retaining selected book text, approved profiles and rule settings | Not "Export DOCX"; not proven cloud backup | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Restore from backup** | Stage, inspect and explicitly approve recovery of archived state | Not import an arbitrary DOCX, not overwrite automatically | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Delete book** | Permanently remove a book subject to actual retention/recovery policy | Remove from list, archive, unpublish | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
| **Sign in / Sign out** | Current authentication UI actions | Log in/log out variants except quotations | `EXISTING_UI` |
| **Create account** | Current sign-up action | Request access if registration actually produces account | `EXISTING_UI` |
| **Account settings** | Settings for signed-in user | Admin area, team permissions | `PROPOSED_LABEL`; route unimplemented |
| **AI settings** | Optional key/model connection settings | Mandatory onboarding/billing console | `PROPOSED_LABEL`; route unimplemented |
| **Privacy** | Explanations/controls for owned content and authorized data transfer | Claiming absolute secrecy or immediate token revocation | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
| **Publishing** | Future professional book composition/distribution-related functions | Ordinary DOCX export, first-MVP menu | `FUTURE_RESERVED` |
| **Cover Studio** | Future editable, layered cover-design capability | Current author-editor mode | `FUTURE_RESERVED`; broader vision preserved |
| **MCP integrations** | Future controlled external assistant interoperability | AI provider selection/connected key | `FUTURE_RESERVED` / `INTERNAL_ONLY` until explicitly enabled |

## 5. Feature and module naming policy

### 5.1 Accepted product areas vs visible navigation

`M1-M8` are planning/acceptance group IDs, **not** eight compulsory tabs. They do not require that the product literally display "Trust and usability" or "Approval and reversibility" in navigation.

| Scope ID | Domain name (approved meaning) | Candidate UI grouping | Live now? |
|---|---|---|---|
| M1 | Books and chapters | **Your books**  **Book workspace**, **Chapters** | Basic `/books` yes; chapter workspace no |
| M2 | Manual manuscript editing | **Manuscript** / **Write** | No integrated editor in `main` |
| M3 | Draft durability and portability | **Revision history**, **Export and backup** | No full editor revision/recovery UI |
| M4 | Live Story Profiles | **Story Workspace**  **Characters**, **Story threads**, **Suggested facts** | No |
| M5 | Simplified Rules Studio | **Rules Studio** | No |
| M6 | Optional bounded AI | Contextual **Continue writing**, **Rewrite selection**; **AI settings** | No |
| M7 | Review, approval, reversibility | **Review suggestion**, **Review facts**, **Undo** | No actual DB-safe flows |
| M8 | Privacy and comfortable use | **Account settings**, **Privacy**, contextual notices | Login/library only |

**Key guidance:** Don't introduce "AI Studio" or "Content Generator" as a large new MVP module without a scope/design decision. AI commands should be contextual to the editor; connection preferences belong in settings.

### 5.2 Feature name casing

Use **Rules Studio**, **Story Profiles**, **Story Workspace** and **Cover Studio** when referring to those proposed/branded Vellistry feature names; use sentence case for general feature labels (**Revision history**, **Account settings**, **Story threads**). Do not Title Case every UI action. `Rules Studio` is a name, not an instruction that all child headings be capitalized.

### 5.3 No duplication of workspace vocabulary

- **Book workspace** = the environment for one book (planning/design proposal).
- **Manuscript editor** = where accepted prose is written (accepted concept).
- **Story Workspace** = proposed supporting-story information view.
- **Rules Studio** = writing guidance configuration.
- **AI settings** = optional connection settings.

Do not name all five "Editor", "Workspace", "Studio", or "Dashboard". Avoid creating a separate **AI Studio** just to hold two contextual commands.

## 6. Navigation, pages, headings and breadcrumbs

### 6.1 Verified current routes (checked in `main`)

| Route | Existing visible title/copy | Recommendation and status |
|---|---|---|
| `/` | **Vellistry**, "Write and organize your fiction. AI is optional."; sign-in and books links | **Keep** brand, plain product descriptor, manual-first promise (`EXISTING_UI`); foundation disclaimer appropriate to internal dev only |
| `/login` | **Sign in to Vellistry**, **Email**, **Password**, **Sign in**, **Create account**, **Back to home** | **Keep** names; improve ambiguous sign-up result and error handling when approved |
| `/books` | **Your books**, **New book title**, **Create**, **Home**, **Refresh**, **Try again**, **Save again anyway**, **Sign out** | Keep **Your books**, **Sign out**; propose **Book title**, **Create book** and explicit uncertain-create confirmation |
| `/api/books` | Server API, not page | Never expose as navigation or use transport error enum verbatim |

### 6.2 Proposed future author navigation - **not implemented routes**

| Placement | Recommended label | Page heading | Evidence/state |
|---|---|---|---|
| Primary collection | **Your books** | Your books | Existing UI |
| Inside a book | **Manuscript** | Author's chapter title, with **Manuscript** context | Proposed layout consistent with M2 |
| Inside a book | **Chapters** | Chapters | Approved chapter workflow; visible label proposed |
| Inside a book | **Story Workspace** | Story Workspace | Proposed design label, not a route approval |
| Story Workspace subview | **Characters** | Characters | Approved concept, proposed UI location |
| Story Workspace subview | **Story threads** | Story threads | Name mapping pending between Storyline and Thread |
| Story Workspace subview | **Suggested facts** | Suggested facts | Proposed fact-review label |
| Inside a book | **Rules Studio** | Rules Studio | Approved feature name |
| Book menu | **Export and backup** | Export and backup | Proposed grouping; actions remain distinct |
| Global user menu | **Account settings** | Account settings | Proposed name |
| Account settings | **AI settings** | AI settings | Proposed name |
